In the first part of the law proposal, arrangements are made to provide incentives to employees and employers, to pay online students a net minimum monthly salary in medical and dental schools, to encourage electricity production from renewable energy sources in housing, to encourage investment in capital structures, to promote Turkish lira instruments, to support individual participants, to reduce non-regulation, and to prevent unfair commercial gains. As it is known, young people who are struggling with the repayment of their student loans and contributions during the course of the study ask that the current application be changed and eased in their debt payments, which has caused the increase by folding their loans. In the current application, loans given to students are set to be repaid by applying the Toptan Purchase Price Index, and when a debt is due due to unemployment, the debt amount is postponed by indexing with domestic Hume. In December of last year, Mr. President said that they would discuss this issue, that they would step in favor of our young people, and in July, he announced that they agreed that the loan repayment would only be done through the amount of credits taken without any inflation or interest application. The National Movement Party has also shared with the public on every occasion the view of the additions to our young people's KYK loan loans to be expunged and paid off. The law proposal changes the method of debt computing and repayment in student loans, and the provisions for debt which is not paid on time are revised. According to this, the application for TEFE and IPFE index on student loans is being lifted and the more loans the student has received, the more debt is being held. A student who uses a loan must pay off his debt in the same amount of time and monthly periods as he has received a loan two years after graduating from the educational institution or for any reason leaving the teaching institution. The loan is allowed to postpone its debt three times at the most. When the loan is requested for a postponement, the loan will be calculated by adding 10 per cent each year in the first year, without any addition in the first year, and in the later years. However, this rate will be applied in the following year, if the consumer price index is less than 10 per cent. If the payment meets the military term, the loan will be postponed until the end of the military term, without any additional notice, at the request of the debtor. Loan debt, which is not useful in pursuing it for financial or legal reasons and does not exceed the amount shown in budget laws, will be abandoned. The overdue installments will be calculated and followed according to Law 6183. The remaining debts of those who have died or who have been disabled 90 per cent or more will be abandoned during the study or after the study. In addition, the amount of indexes still taking loans, which have not yet started paying loans, will also be abandoned in installments on unpaid debts of those who continue to pay loans.
